To tackle the issues, in this paper, we propose Enhanced Deformable ConvNets (EDCN) for semantic segmentation. Specifically, a novel Enhanced Deformable Convolution (EDC) is exploited in the decoder, which integrates the Center-invariant Offset Module (COM) and Edge-aware Mask Module (EMM). The COM employs larger kernels and eliminates deformations at the kernel center, obtaining offsets that are more in line with the target from richer spatial information. Concurrently, the EMM obtains the significance of image content via Sobel edge detection, then selectively applies deformations based on the content significance, minimizing unnecessary deformations associated with relatively less important information, thereby avoiding impact from less informative regions. Experiments show that EDC outperforms state-of-the-art deformable convolution variants, including Deformable ConvNets V1-V4 and Entire Deformable ConvNets, across mainstream segmentation datasets with various decoder settings. Moreover, ablation studies confirm the effectiveness of each component. In addition, visualizations illustrate that EDC enhances spatial adaptation and target focus. We further analyze the extendibility of EDC to larger kernels on the image classification benchmark. Code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [72] [Advanced Brain Tissue Imaging with Data-Consistent Diffusion Priors in Laminographic X-Ray Nanoimaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10456)
*Wenxuan Fang,Abraham L. Levitan,Ana Diaz,Carles Bosch,Adrian Wanner,Andreas T. Schaefer,Mirko Holler,Tomas Aidukas,Nicholas W. Phillips,Yuxin Zhang,Alexandra Pacureanu,Manuel Guizar-Sicairos,Luis Barba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LUCID框架，结合多视角扩散先验与投影域数据一致性，解决X射线层析成像缺失锥问题，恢复未测傅里叶信息，提升脑组织纳米成像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哺乳动物大脑纳米成像对连接组学至关重要。X射线层析成像可实现高通量成像，但倾斜采集几何导致傅里叶空间覆盖不完整，形成"缺失锥"，传统重建方法无法恢复锥内未测信息，产生伪影并扭曲精细脑结构。直接3D深度学习方法受数据稀缺与计算成本限制。

Method: LUCID（Laminography with Unified Consistent Diffusion）：将多视角扩散先验与投影域数据一致性相结合，融合互补的3D结构信息，同时强制与层析成像前向模型严格对齐。该框架仅在完整采样的断层体积上训练。

Result: 在模拟数据集上，LUCID显著提升空间保真度并恢复缺失的傅里叶分量，性能优于基线方法。应用于实验层析成像数据时，尽管仅在完整采样断层体积上训练，仍表现出稳健的泛化能力，有效恢复未测傅里叶信息。

Conclusion: LUCID通过扩散先验与数据一致性结合，为层析成像缺失锥问题提供了有效的解决方案，展示了在实验数据上的强泛化性，有望推动高通量脑连接组学纳米成像。

Abstract: Nanoscale imaging of mammalian brains is critical for connectomics. X-ray laminography enables high-throughput imaging of extended, plate-like biological specimens. However, the tilted acquisition geometry leads to incomplete Fourier-space coverage, giving rise to a missing-cone of information. Conventional reconstruction methods cannot recover unmeasured information within the cone, resulting in artifacts that distort fine brain structures. While resolving these requires modeling 3D structure, direct 3D deep learning approaches are limited by data scarcity and computational cost. Here we introduce LUCID (Laminography with Unified Consistent Diffusion), a framework that combines multi-view diffusion priors with projection-domain data consistency. LUCID integrates complementary 3D structural information while enforcing strict alignment with the laminography forward model. On simulated datasets, LUCID substantially improves spatial fidelity and restores missing Fourier components, outperforming baseline methods. Applied to experimental laminography data, LUCID generalizes robustly despite being trained exclusively on fully sampled tomographic volumes, and effectively recovers unmeasured Fourier information.

</details>


### [73] [AgroVisNet: A lightweight Convolutional Network and the BD-PlantDX Expert-Validated Benchmark for Radish, Potato and Pointed Gourd Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10469)
*Md. Abdullah Mandal,Saad Ahmed,Md. Khalid Syfullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了轻量级卷积网络 AgroVisNet 及专家验证的孟加拉国田间作物病害基准 BD-PlantDX，在极低参数量下实现高精度识别并支持端侧部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化植物病害诊断多部署于农学专家匮乏、网络不稳定的地区，但现有工作存在三大障碍：公开基准以少数非本地作物为主、区域数据集缺乏领域专家验证、高精度架构参数量不适合低成本硬件。

Method: 构建 AgroVisNet：从头训练的紧凑卷积网络，融合带通道与空间顺序注意力的分组瓶颈残差块、多尺度深度可分离块以及双池化分类头，仅 290,572 个可训练参数；同时构建 BD-PlantDX 基准，包含孟加拉国 Bogura 与 Nilphamari 地区 12,432 张田间图像、覆盖萝卜、马铃薯和瓜类的 12 个类别（健康与患病）。

Result: 在 BD-PlantDX 上达到 99.52% 测试准确率与 99.52% 加权 F1，优于六个 ImageNet 预训练轻量骨干，参数量少 8.7–16.8 倍、乘加运算少 1.3–8.5 倍；量化后为 0.46 MB 全整型网络，精度仅损失 0.22 个百分点，单 CPU 单图推理 8.40 ms；五个随机种子下准确率 99.57 ± 0.10%，十变体消融验证各组件贡献，并零改造迁移到两个独立数据集（98.71% 与 99.05%）。

Conclusion: AgroVisNet 在参数效率、精度与部署可行性之间取得良好平衡，适合资源受限的田间场景；Grad-CAM 证据表明模型关注带病斑的叶片区域而非背景线索，具备较好的可解释性与泛化潜力。

Abstract: Automated plant disease diagnosis is increasingly deployed on farmer-held devices in regions where agronomic expertise is scarce and network connectivity is unreliable. Three obstacles limit its practical value: public benchmarks are dominated by a small set of non-native crops, region-specific datasets are rarely validated by domain experts, and the architectures that reach competitive accuracy carry parameter budgets that are unsuited to low-cost hardware. We propose AgroVisNet, a compact convolutional network trained from scratch, together with BD-PlantDX, an expert-validated benchmark of 12,432 field images spanning 12 classes of radish, potato and pointed gourd in healthy and diseased states, collected across the Bogura and Nilphamari districts of Bangladesh. AgroVisNet couples grouped bottleneck residual blocks carrying sequential channel and spatial attention with multi-scale depthwise blocks and a dual-pooling classification head, reaching 290,572 trainable parameters. On BD-PlantDX the model attains 99.52% test accuracy and 99.52% weighted F1, exceeding all six ImageNet-pretrained lightweight backbones evaluated under an identical protocol while using 8.7 to 16.8 times fewer parameters and 1.3 to 8.5 times fewer multiply-accumulate operations. Exported for deployment, the model quantises to a 0.46 MB full-integer network at a 0.22 percentage-point accuracy cost and classifies an image in 8.40 ms on a single CPU. Across five random seeds accuracy remains at 99.57 +- 0.10%, a ten-variant ablation isolates the contribution of each component, and the same architecture transfers without redesign to two independently collected datasets at 98.71% and 99.05% accuracy. Grad-CAM evidence indicates that predictions rest on lesion-bearing leaf regions rather than on background cues.

</details>


### [74] [Artificial Intelligence Literacy and Sustainable Development: An Ethical Governance and Development Goals Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10489)
*Md. Masudul Islam,Mirza Niaz Morshed,Md. Shafiqul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究将AI素养定位为一种治理能力，提出六层次AI推理与伦理分类法，并构建连接教育、治理与可持续发展的整合框架。基于300名参与者的调查发现：技术意识较强但伦理与治理准备不足，且伦理推理与反思性思维是可持续、可信AI应用的最强预测因素。研究主张将素养能力嵌入课程、制度政策与治理机制，以加速公平且负责任的可持续发展进程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI素养虽提供基础能力，但治理、批判性评估与战略决策等高级能力超出基础素养范畴，需要更高阶的AI胜任力。同时，AI素养有望作为治理能力补充并强化全部17项可持续发展目标（SDGs）。然而，公众在伦理与治理层面的准备程度尚不明确，存在能力缺口。

Method: 1）提出一个六层次的人工智能推理与伦理分类法，扩展传统学习模型，纳入伦理判断与战略前瞻；2）以该分类法为基础构建连接教育、治理与可持续发展的整合框架；3）在某国家背景下对300名来自多元职业背景的参与者开展问卷调查。

Result: 调查显示参与者技术意识较强，但伦理与治理准备度有限，暴露出公众在负责任管理AI方面的关键能力缺口。分析表明，伦理推理与反思性思维是可持续且可信赖AI应用的最强预测因素。

Conclusion: 应将以素养为基础的能力嵌入课程体系、机构政策与治理机制，以加速实现公平、负责任的可持续发展目标；AI素养应被视为一种治理能力，服务于全部17项SDGs。

Abstract: AI literacy provides foundational competencies that support ethical, transparent, and sustainable technological development, although higher-order capabilities such as governance, critical evaluation, and strategic decision-making extend beyond basic literacy into advanced levels of AI competency. This study positions AI literacy as a governance capacity that complements and strengthens all 17 SDGs. It introduces a six-level taxonomy of artificial intelligence reasoning and ethics that extends traditional learning models by incorporating ethical judgement and strategic foresight. This taxonomy forms the foundation of an integrated framework linking education, governance, and sustainable development. A survey of 300 participants from diverse professional backgrounds within a national context which reveals strong technical awareness but limited ethical and governance readiness, highlighting critical gaps in public capacity to manage artificial intelligence responsibly. Findings show that ethical reasoning and reflective thinking are the strongest predictors of sustainable and trustworthy artificial intelligence use. The study proposed to embed literacy-based competencies into curricula, institutional policies, and governance mechanisms to accelerate equitable and responsible progress toward sustainable development goals

</details>


### [75] [Cross-Model Agreement as a Deployment-Time Reliability Signal for Automatic Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10495)
*Siddharth Gupta,Jitin Singla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无参考的裁判式质量估计框架RBQE，通过主分割模型与独立训练裁判模型的一致性来评估息肉分割可靠性，跨架构裁判（SegFormer-B0）达到ROC-AUC 0.960，且仅需一次额外前向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时结肠镜检查推理阶段无法获得真实标注，息肉分割模型可能静默失败，因此需要一种无需参考标注的可靠性估计方法。

Method: RBQE测量主分割模型与独立训练裁判模型在同一图像上的一致性；在含1,223张图像的标准化外部基准（来自四个公开数据集）上评估四种裁判配置，以分离裁判独立性与架构多样性两个设计轴；使用统一的一致性Dice描述符。

Result: 仅随机初始化不同的同架构裁判即可提供有效可靠性信号（ROC-AUC=0.923）；跨架构裁判更优，SegFormer-B0最强（ROC-AUC=0.960），显著优于同架构对照与UNet++，并在相同协议下超出代表性测试时增强基线0.055 ROC-AUC；提示耦合的MedSAM裁判尽管架构差异最大但表现较差。排除空掩膜后，SegFormer-B0降至0.876（1,046张），同架构对照降至0.783（975张），但RBQE相对两基线的优势在同一子集上反而扩大。随着低一致性样本被逐步拒绝，保留预测的平均Dice提升，支持选择性预测；推理仅需一次额外确定性裁判前向传播。

Conclusion: 跨模型一致性是一种实用且可解释的自动化息肉分割可靠性框架。

Abstract: In real-time colonoscopy, ground-truth annotations are unavailable at inference, so polyp segmentation models can fail silently. We propose Referee-Based Quality Estimation (RBQE), a reference-free framework measuring agreement between a primary segmentation model and an independently trained referee on the same image. RBQE is evaluated on a standardized 1,223-image external benchmark drawn from four public datasets, using four referee configurations chosen to separate two design axes: referee independence and architectural diversity. Using a common Agreement Dice descriptor, a same-architecture referee differing from the primary model only in random initialization already yields a useful reliability signal (ROC-AUC = 0.923), showing that independent training alone is sufficient. Cross-architecture referees improve further: SegFormer-B0 achieves the strongest performance (ROC-AUC = 0.960), significantly outperforming the same-architecture control and UNet++, and exceeding a representative Test-Time Augmentation baseline by 0.055 ROC-AUC under an identical protocol, whereas a prompt-coupled MedSAM referee underperforms despite maximal architectural diversity. Because empty-mask agreement is trivially separable, we also report a restricted evaluation excluding such cases: ROC-AUC falls to 0.876 (SegFormer-B0, 1,046 images) and 0.783 (same-architecture control, 975 images), yet RBQE's margin over both baselines widens on this identical subset. RBQE additionally increases the mean Dice of retained predictions as low-agreement cases are progressively rejected, supporting selective prediction, and requires only one additional deterministic referee forward pass at inference. Our study therefore supports cross-model agreement as a practical, interpretable reliability framework for automated polyp segmentation.

</details>


### [76] [Field Converter: Geometry-Initialized Temporal Residual Refinement for World-Grounded Player Pose Estimation from Soccer Broadcasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10498)
*Simon Khan,Laurent Gajny,Jennyfer Lecompte,Sébastien Laporte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Field Converter 框架，从标定后的足球转播视频中在世界坐标系下估计 3D 球员姿态，通过几何初始化加时间残差修正显著降低根节点误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目体育转播中恢复 3D 人体姿态时，难点在于需要把球员定位到共享的度量世界坐标系，而非仅相对自身身体重建。现有方法难以实现世界坐标下的准确根定位。

Method: 几何初始化与时间残差结合：先用相机和球场几何通过射线-地面相交初始化球员根节点，再从姿态、图像、相机及几何线索预测时间残差修正；对比了逐帧 MLP、TCN 和 Transformer 等时间骨干。

Result: 在比赛不重叠的评测序列上，残差修正将根误差从纯几何的 49cm 降至 MLP 的 14cm、TCN 的 10cm，Transformer 为 11cm；世界空间 MPJPE 达 13.2cm。消融显示残差预测优于直接全局根回归，时间上下文比具体时间骨干更重要。失败分析表明腾空动作是地面几何初始化的主要局限。

Conclusion: 几何初始化加时间残差能有效实现世界坐标系下的 3D 球员姿态估计，时间上下文是性能关键，未来需解决腾空等非地面接触场景。

Abstract: Recovering 3D human pose from monocular sports broadcasts remains challenging when players must be localized in a shared metric world coordinate system rather than only reconstructed relative to their own body. We introduce Field Converter, a geometry-initialized temporal residual framework for world-grounded 3D player pose estimation from calibrated soccer broadcasts. Our method first uses camera and pitch geometry to initialize the player root through ray-ground intersection, then predicts a temporal residual correction from pose, image, camera, and geometric cues. On match-disjoint evaluation sequences, residual refinement reduces root error from 49cm with geometry alone to 14cm with a frame-wise MLP and 10cm with a TCN, while a Transformer achieves a comparable 11cm. The resulting world-space MPJPE reaches 13.2cm, and ablations show that residual prediction clearly outperforms direct global-root regression while temporal context matters more than the specific temporal backbone. Failure analysis further identifies airborne motion as the main limitation of the ground-based geometric initialization.

</details>


### [77] [BrainTaskonomy: Learning How to Pretrain and What to Transfer in fMRI Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10518)
*Junfeng Xia,Wenhao Ye,Junxiang Zhang,Jiayu Zuo,Mo Wang,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用测得的迁移关系来组织fMRI基础模型的预训练与下游适配：预训练阶段用Brain-DiT代理估计十个fMRI域的难度与定向促进关系，形成优先级引导的累积域课程；适配阶段通过十五个任务的迁移构建有向taskonomy，并用预算整数规划选择源任务与路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI基础模型日益整合跨脑状态、队列和采集设置的异构数据，但预训练域常被当作扁平混合处理，下游任务也独立适配，忽略了域间与任务间的学习关系。

Method: 预训练时，轻量Brain-DiT代理估计十个fMRI域的难度和定向促进，生成优先级引导的累积域课程，并结合高到低噪声的时间步调度与联合巩固；适配时，对十五个任务进行受控的一阶与高阶迁移，构建有向taskonomy，再用预算整数规划（BIP）选择直接监督源任务及目标专属路径，无需修改骨干网络。

Result: 联合的优先级域与高到低时间步课程使v-NMSE、PSD-NMSE和FC-MSE相对两维统一采样分别降低6.5%、16.3%和10.5%，并在六个域内和域外任务上表现良好；taskonomy揭示非对称、依赖目标的迁移；探索性封存测试显示，当存在高阶路径空间时，BIP策略相比匹配随机控制有更大的描述性增益。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持按测得的迁移关系而非将域与任务视为独立扁平集合来组织fMRI预训练与适配。

Abstract: fMRI foundation models increasingly aggregate heterogeneous data across brain states, cohorts, and acquisition settings, yet pretraining domains are commonly treated as a flat mixture and downstream tasks are adapted independently. We study whether measured learning relations can organize both stages without modifying the backbone. During pretraining, a lightweight Brain-DiT proxy estimates difficulty and directed facilitation across ten fMRI domains, yielding a priority-guided cumulative domain curriculum combined with high-to-low-noise timestep scheduling and joint consolidation. During adaptation, controlled first- and higher-order transfer across fifteen tasks constructs a directed taskonomy, from which budgeted integer programming (BIP) selects directly supervised source tasks and target-specific routes. The joint priority-domain and high-to-low-timestep curriculum reduces v-NMSE, PSD-NMSE, and FC-MSE by 6.5%, 16.3%, and 10.5%, respectively, relative to uniform sampling over both dimensions, and shows strong downstream performance across six in- and out-of-domain tasks. The taskonomy reveals asymmetric, target-dependent transfer, while exploratory sealed-test evaluation shows larger descriptive gains for BIP policies when higher-order route spaces are available than for matched random controls. Together, these findings support organizing fMRI pretraining and adaptation by measured learning relations rather than treating domains and tasks as independent flat sets.

</details>


### [78] [Precision in Rice Variety Classification using Stacking-Based Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10524)
*Md. Masudul Islam,Galib Muhammad Shahriar Himel,Md. Golam Moazzam,Mohammad Shorif Uddin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个基于堆叠集成模型的水稻品种识别框架，构建了包含20个品种的图像数据集，实现100%分类准确率，并集成到手机应用中，供用户通过智能手机拍摄米粒图像识别品种，旨在打击掺假行为、保障水稻品质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水稻品种多样性给消费者、贸易商和农户的准确识别带来困难，容易引发品种非法混掺等欺诈行为，损害供应链质量与信任。现有研究在基于颜色、大小、纹理等外部特征进行精确水稻品种分类方面，尚缺乏稳健高效的方法。

Method: 开发了一个专用于水稻品种分类的堆叠集成模型（stacked ensemble model），并构建了一个包含20个水稻品种、具有独特视觉属性的综合图像数据集；随后将模型集成到移动应用中，支持新手用户用手机相机拍摄米粒图像进行识别。

Result: 所提方法取得了前所未有的100%分类准确率；移动应用使普通用户也能轻松利用手机拍摄的米粒图像识别水稻品种。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了先进机器学习技术在减少欺诈行为、确保严格水稻质量控制方面的变革潜力，对农业相关方具有重要意义，为自动化作物识别系统与精准农业发展提供了路径。

Abstract: Rice, a staple food for a significant portion of the global population, exhibits remarkable diversity in its varieties, presenting substantial challenges for accurate identification by consumers, traders, and farmers. This complexity often facilitates fraudulent practices, such as the unauthorized mixing of rice types, which undermines quality and trust in the supply chain. Despite its critical importance, existing research falls short of providing robust and efficient methods for precise rice variety classification based on external characteristics like color, size, and texture. To address this gap, our study introduces a comprehensive rice variety identification framework designed to enhance transparency and quality assurance. We developed a stacked ensemble model tailored for rice variety classification and curated a comprehensive dataset comprising 20 rice varieties, each distinguished by unique visual attributes. The proposed approach achieved an unprecedented classification accuracy of 100%. Furthermore, we integrated our model into a mobile application, enabling even novice users to effortlessly identify rice varieties using grain images from a smartphone camera. These findings underscore the transformative potential of advanced machine learning techniques in mitigating fraudulent practices and ensuring stringent rice quality control. Our work holds significant implications for agricultural stakeholders, paving the way for automated crop identification systems and advancing precision agriculture practices.

</details>


### [79] [Guiding Image-to-3D Generation with Test-Time Partial Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10531)
*Jerred Chen,Simon Weber,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练框架，通过射线一致观测似然引导预训练图像到3D生成模型，融合部分几何观测以提升几何保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像到3D模型虽能生成视觉上吸引人的3D资产，但几何结构仅受观测约束松散，难以满足需要几何保真度的应用。现实场景测试时可能已有物体的部分几何观测，因此希望利用这些证据。

Method: 提出免训练框架，无需重训或微调即可将几何证据融入预训练图像到3D生成模型。通过在模型占据表示上定义射线一致的观测似然来引导生成，结合表面占据与自由空间证据。应用于SAM 3D及其多视角扩展版本。

Result: 在不同可观测程度下，几何保真度显著提升，视觉质量也得到改善。

Conclusion: 预训练图像到3D模型可通过显式测试时引导有效整合部分几何观测，补充其学习到的生成先验，且无需修改底层模型。

Abstract: Image-to-3D models can generate visually compelling 3D assets from a single RGB image, but their geometry is often only loosely constrained by the available observations, limiting their use in applications that require geometric fidelity. In many real-world settings, however, partial geometric observations of the object may be available at test time. We introduce a training-free framework for incorporating such evidence into pretrained image-to-3D generative models without retraining or finetuning. To do this, we guide generation using a ray-consistent observation likelihood defined over the model's occupancy representation, combining surface occupancy and free-space evidence. Applied to SAM 3D and its multi-view extension, our approach substantially improves geometric fidelity across different levels of observability, as well as visual quality. Our results demonstrate that pretrained image-to-3D models can effectively integrate partial geometric observations through explicit test-time guidance, complementing their learned generative priors without modifying the underlying model.

</details>


### [80] [Programmable World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10540)
*Zheng-Hui Huang,Guixu Lin,Jiacheng Lin,Yi-Chuan Huang,Ruihan Yu,Muyao Niu,Siqi Yang,Yu-Lun Liu,Yung-Yu Chuang,Kaipeng Zhang,Zhixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Programmable World Model 框架，将世界状态演化与视觉观测生成解耦，用可执行程序维护显式持久状态，并通过 state-augmented 3D OBB 中间表示驱动预训练视频模型渲染，实现可编程、持久的世界模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型虽能生成真实交互式视觉体验，但缺乏维护持久世界状态和对长时交互施加可编程规则的可靠机制，无法直接控制单个实体及其交互。

Method: 将自然语言指令翻译为可执行程序，指定实体状态与状态转移规则；轻量引擎执行程序维护显式持久的全局状态（含离屏实体与非视觉属性）；引入 state-augmented 3D oriented bounding boxes (OBBs) 作为中间表示，与目标相机轨迹一起确定性地编译为像素对齐的时空条件信号，驱动预训练视频模型作为生成渲染器。

Result: 在自建基准 CombatStateBench 上，方法达到 94% Count Accuracy 与 98% State Accuracy，显著优于现有交互式视频世界模型，并支持连贯的长时序生成。

Conclusion: 将显式状态演化与生成式渲染分离，能有效构建持久、可编程的世界，支持具有预定义机制的可玩游戏和对单个实体的直接控制。

Abstract: Recent video world models generate increasingly realistic and interactive visual experiences, yet lack reliable mechanisms for maintaining persistent world state and enforcing programmable rules over extended interactions. We introduce Programmable World Model, a framework that decouples world-state evolution from visual observation generation. An agent translates natural-language instructions into executable programs that specify entity states and state-transition rules, enabling direct control over individual entities and their interactions. A lightweight engine executes these programs to update and maintain an explicit, persistent global world state, including off-screen entities and non-visual attributes. To connect world state with visual generation, we introduce state-augmented 3D oriented bounding boxes (OBBs) as an intermediate representation. This representation, together with the target camera trajectory, is deterministically compiled into pixel-aligned spatiotemporal conditioning signals for a pretrained video model serving as the generative renderer. This design allows users to create playable games with predefined mechanics, direct control over individual entities, and persistent world state throughout gameplay. We further introduce CombatStateBench, a benchmark for evaluating programmable world models. On CombatStateBench, our method achieves 94% Count Accuracy and 98% State Accuracy, substantially outperforming existing interactive video world models while supporting coherent long-horizon generation. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of separating explicit state evolution from generative rendering for building persistent, programmable worlds.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [Identifying Habit, Physics, and Nuisance in Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09210)
*Jinting Hang,Zhenhui Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出遥操作演示中的多模态性通常混合了操作者习惯、共享物理和观测干扰三类因素，并用结构因果模型 a=g(h,z,u), z'=f(z,a), o=r(z,c) 对其进行形式化拆分，通过互补干预验证该分解，并提出只更新薄接口、冻结共享物理读出层的适配规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作演示常呈现多模态，但底层动力学在给定动作时近似确定。作者认为这种多模态并非全部来自真实动力学，而是混杂了操作者动作选择习惯、共享物理规律与观测干扰三方面，导致纠缠式的下一观测预测器把这些因素一并吸收，从而损害迁移与适配效果。因此需要显式拆分这些因素。

Method: 用结构因果模型 a=g(h,z,u)、z'=f(z,a)、o=r(z,c) 刻画动作、状态与观测的生成过程，其中 h 表示操作者习惯、c 表示观测干扰。设计互补干预进行检验：在固定状态替换或打乱动作，应显著增加下一状态误差；而改变外观和相机则不应如此。同时引入习惯感知的反向评分，用于在不改写动力学的前提下改进对可行历史状态的排序。适配策略为冻结共享物理读出层，仅更新薄接口。

Result: 在 StackCube、DROID 和 RH20T 上，该适配规则相比从零训练提升了低样本迁移效果，在受损适配数据下保持更干净的动力学表征，并能从本体感知扩展到像素观测，且通过了多视角与多步检验。

Conclusion: 作者明确不将潜在动作等同于操作者习惯，也不以大规模视频生成基准为目标。其贡献在于将遥操作多模态性分解为习惯、物理与观测干扰，并通过因果干预与冻结物理读出、仅更新薄接口的适配规则，提升低样本迁移与动力学表征的稳健性。

Abstract: Teleoperated demonstrations are often multimodal even when the underlying dynamics are nearly deterministic given the executed action. We argue that this multimodality typically mixes three factors--operator habit in action selection, shared physics, and observation nuisance--and that entangled next-observation predictors absorb all three. We formalize the split with a structural causal model a=g(h,z,u), z'=f(z,a), o=r(z,c), and test it with complementary interventions: replacing or shuffling actions at fixed state sharply increases next-state error, whereas appearance and camera changes should not; habit-aware reverse scoring improves ranking of feasible pasts without rewriting the dynamics. The associated adaptation rule is to freeze a shared physics readout and update only a thin interface. On StackCube, DROID, and RH20T this rule improves low-shot transfer relative to training from scratch, retains cleaner dynamics under corrupted adaptation data, and extends from proprioception to pixel observations with multi-view and multi-step checks. We do not equate latent actions with operator habit, and we do not target large-scale video generation benchmarks.

</details>


### [82] [Geometry Conditioning in an Embodied SLM: Training Controls and Robustness Diagnostics in a 0.8B Hybrid Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09213)
*Hao Li,Haofei Sun,Lin He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在0.8B混合语言模型（6.2M可训练参数）的机器人操作任务中，训练时的几何对齐并未带来可靠的性能优势；同时相对坐标策略在坐标变换下保持稳健，但对物体位移的泛化能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究物理状态输入（尤其是几何增量信息）如何影响用于操作任务的混合语言模型，并检验训练时几何对齐是否真正带来收益，同时辨析坐标不变性与物理布局泛化之间的差距。

Method: 基于0.8B混合语言模型，仅训练6.2M参数，在LIBERO-Spatial的三个任务上设计六种条件、三个随机种子、540次留出rollout进行评估；对比将循环衰减门条件化于几何增量、训练时打乱增量、无显式几何、使用相同增量的token适配器以及token-clock条件化；另做鲁棒性测试，比较仅状态的相对坐标策略与四种视觉策略在帧重标记和5cm物体位移下的表现。

Result: 将循环衰减门条件化于几何增量得28.9%成功率，训练时打乱增量反而为36.7%，无显式几何为24.4%；两者评估时均输入正确。token适配器为27.8%，差异随种子波动且不确定。token-clock条件化为11.1%，且有一个种子未收敛。鲁棒性测试中，仅状态相对坐标策略在帧重标记下保持7/10成功，而四种视觉策略在5cm物体位移后最高仅为3/20。

Conclusion: 在该实验方案下，训练时几何对齐未体现出可靠优势，说明坐标不变性与物理布局泛化之间存在明显差距；论文附带episode记录、种子级分析和绘图代码。

Abstract: We study how physical-state inputs affect a 0.8B hybrid language model adapted for manipulation with 6.2M trainable parameters. Six conditions are trained on three LIBERO-Spatial tasks and evaluated over three seeds and 540 held-out rollouts. Conditioning recurrent decay gates on geometric increments yields 28.9% success, compared with 36.7% when those increments are shuffled during training and 24.4% without explicit object/goal geometry. Both geometry policies receive correct inputs at evaluation. A token adapter using the same increments scores 27.8%; differences vary across seeds and remain inconclusive. Token-clock conditioning scores 11.1%, including one seed that fails to converge. In separate robustness tests, a state-only relative-coordinate policy retains 7/10 success under frame relabeling, whereas all four tested visual policies fall to at most 3/20 after a 5 cm object displacement. These results show no reliable advantage from training-time geometric alignment under this recipe and illustrate the gap between coordinate invariance and physical-layout generalization. Episode records, seed-level analyses, and figure-generation code accompany the paper.

</details>


### [83] [Design and Attitude Control of an Underwater Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09217)
*Davide Molinaroli,Mohit Singh,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并验证了一款可复现的水下四足机器人，通过定制防水电机外壳和基于SO(3)的闭环姿态控制器，实现水下姿态跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在陆地上具有良好适应性，但在水下环境中应用有限。将四足运动扩展到水中可实现两栖移动，用于检测、环境监测和灾害响应等场景，因此有必要研究水下四足机器人的设计与控制问题。

Method: 采用聚甲醛塑料加工定制防水电机外壳，并使用现成O型圈和动态轴封实现防水；建立简化动力学模型，描述球形末端执行器所受阻力如何将力矩传递到浮动基座；基于该模型设计基于SO(3)特殊正交群误差表述的闭环姿态控制器；通过仿真和水箱实验进行验证。

Result: 控制器在仿真和水箱实验中均得到评估，机器人能够在滚转、俯仰和偏航方向上跟踪期望姿态设定点。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种可复现的水下四足机器人方案，验证了防水设计与基于SO(3)的姿态控制方法在水下姿态跟踪中的有效性，为水下四足运动研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Legged robots are versatile on land, but their use in underwater environments remains limited. Extending quadruped locomotion to water enables amphibious mobility with applications in inspection, environmental monitoring and disaster response. This paper presents the design, modeling, and experimental validation of a reproducible underwater quadruped robot. The robot is built around custom waterproof motor housings machined from polyoxymethylene plastic, which use off-the-shelf O-rings and dynamic shaft seals. A simplified model is derived to describe the dynamics of this underwater legged system, capturing how drag forces on spherical end effectors transmit torque to the floating base. Building on this model, a closed-loop attitude controller is developed using an error formulation defined on the special orthogonal group SO(3). The controller is evaluated both in simulation and experimentally in a water tank, where the robot tracks desired orientation setpoints in roll, pitch and yaw.

</details>


### [84] [Learning to Fly: Stable Vision-Guided UAV Servoing with Compact Target-Centric Cues and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09234)
*Saurbh Singh Jamwal,Nived Chebrolu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用紧凑的目标中心化观测（12D）替代RGB图像，结合课程学习实现无人机长时程视觉伺服，学习策略在扰动下鲁棒性优于传统视觉伺服控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉引导的无人机强化学习面临策略优化不稳定、探索激进以及高维视觉感知代价高的问题，作者希望用更紧凑的表示与课程训练来提升长时程视觉伺服的可训练性与鲁棒性。

Method: 不直接从RGB图像学习，而是用轻量目标分割提取图像空间偏移与相对深度，再与四旋翼速度和投影重力测量组合成12维策略观测。对比Direct PPO与三种等预算课程策略：视觉课程（逐步扩大目标放置难度）、动力学课程（逐步放宽动作约束与平滑）、联合课程（两者结合）。并进行观测消融与对比调优的传统视觉伺服控制器。

Result: 各策略名义性能相当，跟踪指标上各有优势。消融显示本体感知对稳定飞行关键、图像空间线索对目标对准关键，而显式深度在该设定下并非必要。相比调优的经典视觉伺服控制器，学习策略对强控制与视觉扰动更鲁棒；视觉课程在未见目标运动下性能退化最小。

Conclusion: 紧凑的目标中心化表示可支撑鲁棒的长时程空中视觉伺服；视觉课程训练虽在名义性能上收益有限，但能提升对动态分布偏移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-guided reinforcement learning for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) remains challenging due to unstable policy optimisation, aggressive exploration, and the cost of high-dimensional visual perception. In this work, we investigate long-horizon UAV visual servoing using compact target-centric cues combined with low-dimensional sensor measurements. Rather than learning directly from RGB images, lightweight target segmentation provides image-space offsets and relative depth, which are combined with quadrotor velocity and projected-gravity measurements into a compact 12D policy observation. We compare Direct PPO with three matched-budget curriculum strategies: a Visual curriculum that progressively expands target placement difficulty, a Dynamics curriculum that gradually relaxes action constraints and smoothing, and a Joint curriculum that combines both progressions. All strategies reach comparable nominal performance, with complementary advantages across tracking metrics. Observation ablations show that proprioceptive measurements are critical for stable flight and image-space cues for target alignment, while explicit depth is not necessary for strong performance in the evaluated setting. Against tuned classical visual-servo controllers, learned policies show greater robustness to strong control and visual perturbations, while the Visual curriculum exhibits the smallest degradation under unseen target motion. Overall, the results demonstrate that compact target-centric representations can support robust long-horizon aerial visual servoing and that visual curriculum training can improve robustness to dynamic distribution shifts despite limited gains in nominal performance.

</details>


### [85] [No Free Checker: A Survey of Verifiers for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09250)
*Yang Wan,Xihang Yue,Zhirui Liu,Ziyuan Chu,Shuxun Wang,Yuhan Chen,Xiaonan Jiang,Xukun Zhu,Yubo Dong,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述约150个机器人策略验证器（verifier），提出"可用性"（Availability）与"可信度"（Credibility）两个分析维度，按判断来源分为人类、规则/形式化、学习/预训练、模型内蕴四类，发现可信度随可用性上升而下降——"没有免费的检验器"。文章还梳理了验证器自身的验证方式，并提出九项使验证器主张可检验的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略验证器用途广泛（评估与训练视觉-语言-动作策略），形式多样（成功检测器、奖励模型、运行时监控、安全过滤器、时序逻辑规范等），但缺乏统一的比较框架。作者希望厘清"验证器代价与可靠性"之间的根本张力，并回答"谁来验证验证器"这一问题。

Method: 综述性研究。调查约150个验证器，沿两个属性进行比较：(1) 可用性——判断的成本、在rollout中出现的时间早晚、以及可被请求的密度；(2) 可信度——高分能在多大程度上反映任务完成情况，以及判断被"钻空子"（gameable）和自我服务（self-serving）的程度。按判断来源将验证器分为四大家族进行分析。

Result: 在四类验证器家族中普遍观察到"可信度随可用性上升而下降"的权衡关系；无论判断由谁提供，都不存在免费的检验器（no free checker）。关于验证器自身的验证，文献中出现三种度量方式：与人类标注的一致性、其所训练策略的性能、以及在奖励黑客（reward hacking）情境下的行为表现。

Conclusion: 提出九项使验证器主张可检验的指标，并给出尚待构建的验证器的坐标（研究方向的坐标定位），为未来机器人策略验证器的设计、评估与比较提供参考框架。

Abstract: A verifier for robot policies reads a candidate behavior and returns a score for how well it did, used both to evaluate vision-language-action policies and to train them. Verifiers range from success detectors and reward models to runtime monitors, safety filters, and temporal-logic specifications. We survey roughly 150 verifiers and compare them along two properties. Availability is how much a verdict costs, how early in a rollout the verdict arrives, and how often a verdict can be asked for. Availability rises as verdicts get cheaper, earlier, and denser. Credibility is how much a high score tells us about the task. Credibility falls as the judgment becomes gameable and self-serving. We group the verifiers by who supplies the judgment: human verifiers, rule-based and formal verifiers, learned and pretrained verifiers, and model-intrinsic verifiers. Across the four families, we find that credibility falls as availability rises. Regardless of who supplies the judgment, there is no free checker. We then examine what validates a verifier itself, and how much a high score tells us. Three measures appear in the literature: agreement with human labels, the performance of the policy it trains, and behavior under reward hacking. We close with nine metrics that make a verifier claim checkable, and coordinates for the verifiers still to be built.

</details>


### [86] [AccelMPC: High-Rate, Low-Power FPGA-Accelerated Model Predictive Control for Tiny Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09380)
*Andrea Grillo,Brian Plancher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AccelMPC通过求解算法、数值表示、硬件映射与物理集成的端到端协同设计，实现微型无人机上1 kHz的约束MPC实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型空中机器人需要嵌入式边缘控制性能提升一个数量级。现有缓存式MPC求解器虽能处理敏捷飞行的快速动态与复杂约束，但计算需求对资源受限机器人仍过高，只能以降低的控制频率运行。

Method: 采用端到端协同设计，联合优化求解算法、数值表示、硬件映射与物理集成；将协同设计的FPGA加速ADMM-based MPC求解器与定制6g PCB结合，提供高带宽通信并部署在35g Crazyflie上。

Result: 硬件实验实现1 kHz机载约束MPC（含动态障碍物），求解时间最高提升15.6倍，能量延迟积相比最先进的嵌入式微控制器求解器提升195.4倍；可扩展至超过20,000个优化变量及相近数量约束的优化问题。

Conclusion: AccelMPC证明通过软硬件协同设计可在极小、资源受限的无人机上实现高频约束MPC，且开源PCB设计文件、固件与FPGA求解器代码。

Abstract: Unlocking the potential of tiny aerial robots requires order of magnitude improvements in the performance of embedded edge control. In particular, although recent cached model predictive control (MPC) solvers can handle the fast system dynamics and complex constraints required for agile drone flight, their computational demands remain prohibitive for resource-constrained robots, forcing prior implementations to operate at reduced control rates. AccelMPC overcomes this challenge through an end-to-end co-design approach that jointly optimizes the solver algorithm, numerical representation, hardware mapping, and physical integration. AccelMPC pairs a co-designed FPGA-accelerated alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM)-based MPC solver with a custom 6g PCB, providing high-bandwidth communication for deployment on a 35g Crazyflie. Hardware experiments demonstrate 1 kHz onboard constrained MPC with dynamic obstacles, up to 15.6x faster solve times and 195.4x improvement in energy-delay product over state-of-the-art embedded microcontroller-based solvers, all while scaling to optimization problems with over 20,000 optimization variables and a comparable number of constraints. We release our PCB design files, firmware, and FPGA solver code open source.

</details>


### [87] [A Decade of Bayesian Optimization for Controller Tuning and Robot Learning: Tutorial, Review, and Future Prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09403)
*David Stenger,Paul Brunzema,Johanna Menn,Alexander von Rohr,Angela P. Schoellig,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了贝叶斯优化（BO）在控制器调优与机器人学习中的最新进展，从实践者视角介绍BO搭建方法，讨论其与深度学习等范式的定位，梳理多样BO方法，并指出缺乏控制领域标准基准的问题，进而提出轻量级基准套件与评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年BO已成为自动控制器调优与机器人学习的强大框架，但研究者与实践者缺乏对最新进展、实际应用与未来方向的系统理解，尤其缺少面向控制应用的标准基准问题，难以进行严格评估与公平比较。

Method: 采用综述与统一视角的方法：从实践者角度通过代表性控制器调优示例说明BO的搭建；将BO置于从深度强化学习到数据驱动控制的更广学习范式背景中定位；梳理应对复杂问题与特定应用的多种BO方法；并着手构建面向控制工程与机器人的轻量级基准套件，同时给出评估指标与最佳实践。

Result: 提供了BO当前图景的统一视角，明确其对控制系统与机器人的相关性，识别了关键研究挑战与有前景的方向，并初步建立了轻量级控制工程与机器人基准套件及可直接比较新算法与现有最优方法的指标与最佳实践。

Conclusion: BO在控制与机器人领域具有重要价值，未来需补足标准化基准缺失这一关键空白，通过轻量级基准、统一指标与最佳实践推动严格评估，促进BO算法在该领域的持续发展与可比性研究。

Abstract: In the past decade, Bayesian optimization (BO) has emerged as a powerful and adaptable framework for automatic controller tuning and robot learning. This article offers a comprehensive overview of the state-of-the-art in BO, designed to support both researchers and practitioners in understanding recent advancements, practical applications, and future research directions. We begin by adopting a practitioner's perspective, illustrating how to effectively set up BO through a representative controller tuning example. We position BO within the broader context of learning paradigms, ranging from deep reinforcement learning to data-driven control, and highlight scenarios where BO is most advantageous. Next, we discuss the diverse range of BO methods that have been developed to tackle complex problems and specific applications. This article provides a unified perspective on the current landscape of BO, emphasizing its relevance to control systems and robotics, and it highlights future prospects by identifying key research challenges and promising avenues for advancing BO in the field. This includes addressing a significant gap in the BO landscape: the lack of standardized benchmark problems specifically for control-related applications. To foster future research and ensure rigorous evaluation, we start an effort towards a lightweight benchmark suite for control engineering and robotics. We also present metrics and best practices to facilitate direct comparisons between new BO algorithms and established state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [88] [Actuator Dynamics Curricula for Narrow-Viability Tasks in Legged Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09492)
*Kousheek Chakraborty,Chandan K. Rajendra,Ayham Alharbat,Abeje Y. Mersha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"执行器动力学课程"(Actuator Dynamics Curriculum),通过从高关节刚度开始并随完成的回合长度增长退火到系统辨识值,解决探索轨迹过早终止导致无法收敛的腿式机器人强化学习任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分腿式机器人强化学习任务在标准训练下发散/不收敛,原因是大多数探索轨迹在产生有用梯度信号之前就终止了。这类任务的瓶颈在于终止条件而非奖励信号。

Method: 方法为执行器动力学课程:初始化关节刚度为高值,随完成的回合长度增长将其退火至系统辨识值。以倒立摆(cart-pole)为代表系统进行分析,验证临界阻尼下更高的闭环关节自然频率会扩大底层MDP的可生存核(viability kernel);随后将该课程应用于Spot四足机器人的四足到倒立过渡任务。

Result: 在倒立摆上验证了可生存核的单调性;在波士顿动力Spot上,固定辨识刚度的训练仅收敛于永远无法完成过渡的策略,而所提课程训练出的策略在仿真中跨10个随机种子成功执行该过渡,并成功迁移到硬件。

Conclusion: 结果表明,仿真执行器动力学是设计课程的有用维度,适用于探索受终止条件而非奖励信号瓶颈限制的任务。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has produced capable controllers across a broad range of legged-robot tasks, but a subset of these tasks fail to converge under standard training: those for which most exploration trajectories terminate before producing useful gradient signal. To address such tasks we introduce the \emph{Actuator Dynamics Curriculum}, a procedure that initializes joint stiffness at a high value and anneals it toward the system-identified value as completed episode lengths grow. Using a cart-pole system as a representative example, we show that higher closed-loop joint natural frequency under critical damping enlarges the viability kernel of the underlying Markov Decision Process, increasing the fraction of initial states from which the task is feasible. We validate the kernel monotonicity on the cart-pole and apply the curriculum to a quadrupedal-to-handstand transition on the Boston Dynamics Spot, a narrow-viability task where training under fixed identified stiffness plateaus at a policy that never completes the transition. The trained policy executes the transition in simulation across 10 seeds and transfers to hardware. More broadly, our results suggest that simulated actuator dynamics is a useful axis along which to design curricula for tasks in which exploration is bottlenecked by termination conditions rather than by reward signal.

</details>


### [89] [Agentic AI-enabled Semantic Commissioning of a Cognitive Digital Twin for Reconfigurable Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09503)
*Yangyang Liu,Xun Xu,Jan Polzer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于智能体与AI的自动化工作流，利用LangGraph多智能体编排实现认知数字孪生的端到端自动调试，在机器人加工单元验证中感知mAP达97.2%，部署周期从数周缩短至约2小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重构制造中认知数字孪生（CDT）的快速定制化调试是一大难题。传统数字孪生构建方法主要聚焦几何重建，忽视了自主推理所需的深层语义集成与功能互操作性。

Method: 提出基于智能体、由AI驱动的自动化端到端CDT调试工作流。以LangGraph作为多智能体编排引擎实现双路径合成：语义路径通过检索增强生成（RAG）从非结构化文档中提取技术规格；功能路径利用模型上下文协议（MCP）自主发现并绑定实时工业遥测数据。

Result: 在机器人加工单元的实验验证中，系统在感知任务上取得97.2%的平均精度均值（mAP），并将部署周期从数周缩短至平均约2小时。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了从人工脚本编写到自主编排的范式转变，为认知数字孪生的快速定制化部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Rapid bespoke commissioning of the Cognitive Digital Twin (CDT) is a major challenge in reconfigurable manufacturing. Traditional digital twin (DT) construction methods primarily focus on geometric reconstruction, often neglecting the deep semantic integration and functional interoperability necessary for autonomous reasoning. This paper proposes an agent-based, AI-driven workflow to automate end-to-end CDT debugging. The system utilises LangGraph as a multi-agent orchestration engine to achieve dual-path synthesis: the semantic path extracts technical specifications from unstructured documents using Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG), while the functional path autonomously discovers and binds to real-time industrial telemetry data using Model Context Protocol (MCP). Experimental validation in a robotic machining cell demonstrates that the system achieves a mean average accuracy (mAP) of 97.2% in perception and reduces the deployment cycle from several weeks to an average of 2 hours, marking a paradigm shift from manual scripting to autonomous orchestration.

</details>


### [90] [Compact Visuotactile World Models for Lifting: Prediction, Reward Alignment, and Force Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09597)
*Qinzhen Ma,Sida Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究用随机初始化的视觉-触觉世界模型、轨迹级不确定性校准与行为初始化的想象式actor-critic学习，探究接触预测是否真正支持机器人操作决策。结果显示提升传感精度或任务奖励并不等于提升力约束下的控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文的动机在于：精确的接触预测只有在能支撑有效决策时才对机器人操作有用，因此需要研究"预测精度"与"控制决策效果"之间的联系，并区分传感改进、任务奖励改进与力约束控制改进这三者。

Method: 方法包括：紧凑且随机初始化的视觉-触觉世界模型；轨迹级不确定性校准；行为初始化的actor-critic在想象中学习。在160个MuJoCo Lift回合上以三个训练种子评估端点力与区间峰值预测误差，并与触觉持续性基线对比。控制实验进行两轮探索性控制，共680次执行、40个独立测试初始条件，并引入匹配的奖励修订。另在公开GelSight记录上训练力回归器并比较帧级与轨迹级校准。

Result: 加入触觉使端点力预测误差从1.058 N降至0.228 N，区间峰值误差从2.724 N降至0.523 N；但触觉持续性误差更低（0.095 N与0.498 N）。奖励修订后，分布内10 cm举升成功率从20.0%升至93.3%，而8 N每指预算内成功率仅33.3%，力反馈为70.0%。校准裕度可减少力违规但牺牲任务完成度。GelSight力回归误差0.04234 N，帧级校准仅覆盖15.80%完整轨迹，轨迹级校准升至87.36%（名义90%覆盖）。

Conclusion: 研究区分了传感精度提升与任务奖励改进同力约束控制改进之间的差异，表明接触预测精度并不自动转化为更好的约束控制。结论受限于公开传感数据与仿真执行，且未证明二者之间的迁移性。

Abstract: Accurate contact prediction is useful for robotic manipulation only if it supports effective decisions. We investigate this connection using a compact, randomly initialized visuotactile world model, trajectory-level uncertainty calibration, and behavior-initialized actor-critic learning in imagination. On 160 MuJoCo Lift episodes, adding touch reduces endpoint-force prediction error from 1.058 to 0.228 N and interval-peak error from 2.724 to 0.523 N across three training seeds. However, tactile persistence achieves lower errors of 0.095 and 0.498 N, respectively. Two exploratory control rounds comprise 680 executions on 40 independent test initial conditions. A matched reward revision on fresh test environments increases in-distribution 10 cm lifting success from 20.0% to 93.3%, while success within an 8 N per-finger budget reaches only 33.3%, compared with 70.0% for force feedback. Calibration margins reduce force violations at the cost of task completion. In a separate study of public GelSight recordings, a force regressor achieves 0.04234 N error, but frame-level calibration covers only 15.80% of complete trajectories; trajectory-level calibration raises this to 87.36% at nominal 90% coverage. Together, these findings distinguish improvements in sensing and task reward from improvements in force-constrained control. The evidence is limited to public sensing records and simulator execution, without a demonstrated transfer between them.

</details>


### [91] [MuJoCable: Reduced-Order Surface-Routed Cable Transmission for Tendon-Driven Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09612)
*Yi Zhang,Qi Shao,Yicong Lin,Muyuan Ma,Tao Sun,Yue Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MuJoCable：将降阶、构型依赖的缆索传动集成到 MuJoCo，联合求解运动非圆接触、单向张力与分段摩擦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱传动可降低远端惯量并增加顺应性，但布线、松弛与摩擦主导运动与力的传递。主流刚体仿真器（如 MuJoCo）无法同时处理运动的非圆接触、单向张力与分段摩擦，导致仿真与现实存在差距。

Method: 提出 MuJoCable，为 MuJoCo 增加降阶、构型依赖的缆索传动模型。其布线算法在运动的解析与网格曲面上联合优化有序路径；用单向轴向本构、方向性 Capstan 传播与节点虚功将路径映射为分段张力与刚体受力；通过热启动引擎插件在仿真中施加这些力并暴露路径与负载状态。

Result: 滑轮基准恢复了解析传动关系，Capstan 比值误差低于 0.5%。在 18 关节欠驱动 SpiRobs 上，MuJoCable 揭示了摩擦驱动的负载增长与关节转动的近端再分配，而原生腱模型无法表达。在 SpiRobs 与腱路耦合手指上的硬件测试复现了观测到的运动序列。

Conclusion: 通过使物理穿线可执行，MuJoCable 在制造前将传动层面的仿真到现实差距来源纳入路径、缆索与执行器设计。

Abstract: Tendon transmissions reduce distal inertia and add compliance, yet routing, slack, and friction govern motion and force transfer. Mainstream rigid-body robotics simulators such as MuJoCo do not jointly resolve moving noncircular contact, unilateral tension, and segment friction. We present MuJoCable, which adds a reduced-order, configuration-dependent cable transmission to MuJoCo. Its routing algorithm jointly optimizes an ordered path across moving analytic and mesh surfaces. A unilateral axial law, directional Capstan propagation, and nodal virtual work map this path to segment tensions and body forces. The warm-started engine plugin applies these forces during simulation and exposes route and load states for design. Pulley benchmarks recover analytical transmission relations with a Capstan-ratio error below 0.5%. On the underactuated 18-joint SpiRobs, MuJoCable reveals friction-driven load growth and proximal redistribution of joint rotation that the native tendon does not represent. Hardware tests on SpiRobs and a tendon-route-coupled finger reproduce observed motion sequences. By making physical threading executable, MuJoCable brings transmission sources of the simulation-to-reality gap into route, cable, and actuator design before fabrication.

</details>


### [92] [JEPA Policy: Diffusion-Free Imitation Learning via Paired Action and Future Representation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09630)
*Jie Xu,Kangjin Yu,Ziyi Jin,Junjie Gao,Liqing Chen,Yixian Li,Shuai Tian,Zhongpu Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 JEPA Policy，一种免扩散的模仿学习框架，将动作块与观测到的未来表征作为配对训练目标，在共享 Transformer 中交互并两次前向精炼，实现低延迟视觉运动模仿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准行为克隆只监督动作，未显式约束与每个演示动作块配对的未来表征，因此缺乏对未来状态的显式建模。作者希望在不依赖迭代生成采样（如扩散）的前提下，通过配对未来表征监督提升策略性能并保持低延迟。

Method: JEPA Policy 为免扩散框架：动作块与其观测到的未来表征作为配对训练目标，动作 token 与未来表征 token 在共享 Transformer 中交互，经两次前向传播精炼，使未来预测能塑造生成动作所用的表征。并设置双分支与梯度路由对照，以验证增益来自共享拓扑而非单独的辅助预测头。

Result: 在九个仿真任务中，平均成功率优于仅动作的 MIP 基线，并在评估配置下超过 Diffusion Policy，模型延迟仅增加 0.29 ms。五任务、630 回合的实体机器人研究得到一致的汇总排名。审计未发现动作监督下的完全表征坍缩，并发现未来预测误差中存在任务条件下的失败排序信号。

Conclusion: 配对未来表征监督是一种实用的低延迟视觉运动模仿方法，无需迭代生成采样，且增益源于共享拓扑结构而非单一辅助预测头。

Abstract: Standard behavior cloning supervises actions without explicitly constraining the future representation paired with each demonstrated action chunk. We introduce JEPA Policy, a diffusion-free framework that uses the action chunk and its observed future representation as paired training targets. Action and future-representation tokens interact in a shared Transformer and are refined through two forward passes. Future prediction can therefore shape the representation used to generate actions. Dual-branch and gradient-routing controls attribute the gain to this shared topology rather than to an auxiliary prediction head alone. Across nine simulated tasks, JEPA Policy improves mean success over the action-only MIP baseline and outperforms Diffusion Policy under the evaluated configurations, while adding 0.29 ms to MIP's model latency. A five-task, 630-episode physical-robot study produces the same pooled ranking. Further audits find no complete representation collapse under action supervision and identify a task-conditioned failure-ranking signal in future-prediction error. These results support paired future-representation supervision as a practical approach to low-latency visuomotor imitation without iterative generative sampling.

</details>


### [93] [A Risk-Sensitive and Uncertainty-Aware Decision-Making and Control Framework for Safe and Robust Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09650)
*Zhuoren Li,Ran Yu,Weiqi Zhang,Ming Liu,Lu Xiong,Chen Sun,Bo Leng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出风险敏感且不确定性感知的决策与控制框架RUDC，用于城市无信号交叉口安全鲁棒的自动驾驶，在安全、效率与鲁棒性间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在城市自动驾驶特别是高交互无信号交叉口部署困难，学习策略难以兼顾安全与鲁棒决策；传统安全过滤方法采用固定保守约束，虽提升安全却带来过度干预和交通效率下降。

Method: 将风险敏感分布式RL与基于集成的策略不确定性量化耦合，同时考虑回报分布的尾部风险与策略不确定性；采用不确定性感知的高阶控制屏障函数（HOCBF）安全修正机制，根据策略不确定性自适应调整约束严格程度；并设计可学习残差预测器补偿CBF模型失配与离散化误差。

Result: 在无信号交叉口的大量仿真表明，RUDC在安全性、效率与鲁棒性之间实现良好平衡，在正常运行及具有挑战性的OOD和长尾场景下均优于代表性安全RL基线，并满足实时性要求。

Conclusion: RUDC通过结合风险敏感分布式RL、策略不确定性量化与自适应HOCBF安全修正，有效解决了固定保守约束导致的效率与安全矛盾，为城市自动驾驶提供安全鲁棒的决策与控制方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has demonstrated considerable potential for autonomous driving decision-making. However, its deployment in urban autonomous driving, particularly at highly interactive unsignalized intersections, remains challenging, as learned policies may struggle to maintain both safety and robust decision-making in complex traffic situations. Conventional safety-filtering approaches typically employ fixed conservative constraints, which may improve safety at the cost of excessive intervention and degraded traffic efficiency. To address these limitations, we propose a Risk-sensitive and Uncertainty-aware Decision-making and Control (RUDC) framework for safe and robust autonomous driving. RUDC couples risk-sensitive distributional RL with ensemble-based policy uncertainty quantification, jointly accounting for tail risks in return distributions and uncertainty in learned policies. An uncertainty-aware high-order control barrier function (HOCBF)-based safety correction mechanism adaptively adjusts constraint strictness according to policy uncertainty, while a learnable residual predictor compensates for CBF model mismatches and discretization errors. Extensive simulations at unsignalized intersections demonstrate that RUDC achieves a favorable balance among safety, efficiency, and robustness, outperforming representative safe RL baselines under both nominal and challenging OOD and long-tail scenarios while satisfying real-time requirements.

</details>


### [94] [CT-SAFR: Safe and Interpretable Chain-of-Thought Reasoning for Autonomous Robots: A Multi-Layered Verification Framework for Trustworthy AI-Driven Robotic Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09692)
*Cagri Temel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CT-SAFR多层验证框架，用于检测机器人链式推理中的幻觉，幻觉检测率达94.2%，延迟低于500ms，仓库机器人案例中不安全推理输出减少87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维（CoT）提示让大模型能够显式逐步推理，为自主机器人带来新机遇，但近期研究发现推理模型仅在25-39%的情况下真实表达其实际决策过程，在复杂任务上忠实度还下降44%，存在严重安全隐患，因此需要一套面向机器人的安全与忠实性验证机制。

Method: 提出CT-SAFR（面向机器人的链式思维安全与忠实性）多层验证框架，通过多层次校验检测推理链中的幻觉与不安全输出，并以仓库机器人为案例进行验证（n=500，报告95%置信区间）。

Result: 幻觉检测率达到94.2%（n=500，95% CI: 91.8-95.9%），延迟低于500ms；在仓库机器人案例中不安全推理输出减少87%（p < 0.001）。

Conclusion: CT-SAFR在低延迟下实现了高幻觉检测率并显著降低不安全推理输出，为具备推理能力的自主机器人负责任部署提供了可行建议。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting enables LLMs to perform explicit, step-by-step reasoning, creating opportunities for sophisticated autonomous robots. However, recent research reveals that reasoning models verbalize their actual decision processes only 25-39% of the time, with faithfulness degrading 44% on complex tasks. This paper presents CT-SAFR (Chain-of-Thought Safety and Faithfulness for Robotics), a multi-layered verification framework achieving 94.2% hallucination detection (n = 500, 95% CI: 91.8-95.9%) with sub-500ms latency. Through a warehouse robot case study, this work demonstrates 87% reduction in unsafe reasoning outputs (p < 0.001) and provides recommendations for responsible deployment of reasoning-capable autonomous robots.

</details>


### [95] [PccDiffuser: Multi-solution Motion Planning for Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09745)
*Ke Qiu,Sifan Chen,Si Wang,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang,Haojian Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 PccDiffuser，一个面向连续体机器人的条件扩散规划框架，学习配置空间路径的多模态分布并并行采样多个候选解，再结合执行器约束进行时间分配生成可执行轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人在复杂环境中的规划面临多模态解空间与执行器约束问题，现有采样与优化方法在成功率和计算效率上存在不足。

Method: 在分段常曲率模型下用指数坐标描述运动学，采用图神经网络编码可变数量的障碍物，并在去噪过程中引入解析微分运动学以提升末端精度与全身间隙；采样得到的候选解通过考虑执行器约束的时间分配转换为可执行轨迹。

Result: 在含零至四个障碍物的混合测试集上成功率达 91%；与现有采样和优化基准相比，成功率更高、计算效率更优，且候选解采样数量增加时效率优势更明显。在三段腱驱动连续体机器人实验上验证了连续规划、多解规划与全身避障能力。

Conclusion: PccDiffuser 为连续体机器人提供了一种高效、多模态且满足执行约束的规划方案，具有实际机器人平台的可部署性。

Abstract: We present the PccDiffuser, a conditional diffusion framework for continuum robots that learns a multimodal distribution over complete configuration-space paths and samples multiple candidate solutions in parallel, which are subsequently converted into an executable trajectory by time allocation considering actuator constraints. Under the piecewise constant-curvature model, we use exponential co-ordinates to describe the robot kinematics, and use graph neural network to encode a variable number of environment obstacles. Analytical differential kinematics is incorporated in the denoising process to improve terminal accuracy and whole-body clearance. On a mixed test set comprising workspace with zero to four obstacles, PccDiffuser achieved a success rate of 91\%. Compared with existing sampling- and optimisation-based benchmarks, it delivered both a higher success rate and greater computational efficiency, with the latter advantage becoming more substantial when sampling more candidate solutions. Experiments on a three-section tendon-driven continuum robot further demonstrate consecutive planning, multi-solution planning, and whole-body obstacle avoidance.

</details>


### [96] [HiRAD: A Flexible Large-Scale AGV Routing System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09752)
*Yunjie Huang,Ruizhong Wu,Mengxuan Zhang,Frodo Kin Sun Chan,Yan Nei Law,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HiRAD——面向连续空间AGV路由的分层强化学习框架，通过时空表示、分层策略与异步事件驱动决策，实现实时、可扩展的多智能体路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AGV车队路由困难：经典多智能体路径规划（MAPF）组合复杂度爆炸、运行时间超二次方，且依赖理想化网格或分段线性运动模型；现有RL方法依赖离散时空表示、需数百万回合收敛、每步需全图观测，导致模型大、收敛慢、推理延迟高，难以满足工业实时控制要求。

Method: HiRAD采用三项设计：(1) 步级时空表示，将连续运动转化为可微RL问题；(2) 分层策略，将航向选择与速度控制解耦以缩小动作空间；(3) 异步事件驱动决策流水线，将推理复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n)。

Result: 每步延迟最多降低71%；在随机图与两个仓库地图上，makespan减少45%至63%，并缩短端到端运行时间。

Conclusion: HiRAD在连续空间中兼顾可扩展性与实时性，显著优于经典MAPF与现有RL方法，适合工业级AGV路由。

Abstract: Automatic Guided Vehicles (AGVs) substantially boost warehouse throughput, but routing large-scale AGV fleets remains challenging. Classical Multi-Agent Pathfinding solvers suffer from exploding combinatorial complexity and super-quadratic runtime, while relying on idealized grid or piecewise-linear motion models that mismatch real-world kinematics. Recent Reinforcement Learning (RL) solutions improve flexibility via decentralized agent policies but depend on discretized spatiotemporal representations, require millions of episodes to converge, and incur full-map observation at every step, which leads to large models, slow convergence, and high inference latency that violates real-time industrial control constraints. To address these bottlenecks, we propose HiRAD, a hierarchical RL framework for continuous-space AGV routing with real-time guarantees: (1) a step-level spatiotemporal representation that translates continuous motion into a differentiable RL problem, (2) a hierarchical strategy that splits heading choice from velocity control to reduce the action space, and (3) an asynchronous event-driven decision pipeline that lowers inference complexity from O(n^2) to O(n) and cuts per-step latency by as much as 71 percent. Across random graphs and two warehouse maps, HiRAD reduces makespan by 45 percent to 63 percent and shortens end-to-end runtime.

</details>


### [97] [GTA-2: A Multi-VLM Framework for Synthesizing Robot Manipulation Skills via Grounded Task Axes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09808)
*M. Yunus Seker,Shobhit Aggarwal,Ruwan Wickramarachchi,Jonathan Francis,Oliver Kroemer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GTA-2多VLM模块化框架，从可复用物体中心任务轴部件构建可执行、任务定制的操作技能，14个真机任务上零样本成功率73.9%，定向精修后达90.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作任务常被分解为行为或技能，但需针对特定任务预定义，或仅用通用技能覆盖广泛任务。这导致这些行为往往过于粗糙，无法暴露执行所需的几何、控制及场景相关决策。

Method: GTA-2将每个技能表示为语义子任务，包含任务相关关键点与轴、控制器组合及场景相关参数。四个专用VLM智能体分别负责任务分解、构建抽象任务轴技能、分配控制器参数、从RGB-D观测中接地所需视觉特征。这种"抽象到接地"的因子化支持无需任务演示、策略训练或微调的零样本技能生成，并使中间决策显式化以支持针对性人工反馈。

Result: 在14个真机操作任务上与VLA策略pi_{0.5}及两个Code-as-Policies基线比较，GTA-2零样本平均成功率73.9%，超出最强基线31.4个百分点；定向精修后平均成功率提升至90.7%。

Conclusion: GTA-2通过抽象-接地因子化和可复用任务轴部件，实现了无需训练的可执行技能生成，性能显著优于VLA与Code-as-Policies基线，且支持组件级人工精修。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation tasks are often decomposed into behaviors or skills. However, one often needs to predefine these behaviors for specific tasks or try to cover a wide range of tasks using generic skills. As a result, these behaviors can remain too coarse to expose the geometric, control, and scene-dependent decisions required for execution. We introduce Grounded Task Axes v2 (GTA-2), a modular multi-VLM framework that constructs executable, task-bespoke manipulation skills from reusable object-centric task-axis components. Rather than predicting actions end-to-end or composing fixed task-level primitives, GTA-2 represents each skill as semantic subtasks comprising task-relevant keypoints and axes, controller compositions, and scene-dependent parameters. Four specialized VLM agents separately decompose the task, construct an abstract task-axis skill, assign controller parameters, and ground the required visual features from RGB-D observations. This abstraction-to-grounding factorization enables zero-shot skill generation without task-specific robot demonstrations, policy training, or fine-tuning. It also keeps intermediate decisions explicit, allowing targeted human feedback to refine an incorrect stage while preserving correct components. We evaluate GTA-2 on 14 real-robot manipulation tasks against a VLA policy pi_{0.5} and two Code-as-Policies baselines using task-axis controllers or conventional robot primitives. GTA-2 achieves an average zero-shot success rate of 73.9%, exceeding the strongest baseline by 31.4 percentage points, while targeted refinement raises GTA-2's average success rate to 90.7%. Project page: https://gta2-project.github.io/

</details>


### [98] [ViBe: Visual Behavior Adaptation for Perceptive Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09918)
*Lokesh Krishna,Sarvesh Venkatesan,An Zhang,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViBe：将运动跟踪器通过低秩适配器嫁接预训练视觉编码器，实现参数高效微调，完成感知型全身控制任务的零样本sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动跟踪为人形全身控制提供了可扩展方案，但其设计上缺乏外部感知反馈，环境响应只能依赖上层规划器。现有感知控制器从零训练仅含几何信息的编码器，牺牲语义换取sim-to-real便捷性，且通常依赖教师-学生蒸馏针对特定任务。

Method: 提出ViBe后训练框架：利用预训练视觉编码器与多查询提取器模块学习任务相关的感知反馈，通过低秩适配器将该反馈嫁接到跟踪器输入端，实现参数高效微调；给定任务奖励与参考数据集，模块化控制器可直接经策略优化适配。

Result: 在四项任务上实现零样本sim-to-real迁移：路缘与跑酷感知行走、Repose Cube、全物体移动操作、躲避球，并在户外、低光和RGB干扰物条件下表现视觉鲁棒。还用刻意简化的规划器解决了目标导向的Repose Cube任务。

Conclusion: 证明通过预训练视觉编码器嫁接与低秩微调，可将运动跟踪器高效改造为感知控制器，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Motion tracking provides a scalable recipe for humanoid whole-body control. By design, the resulting trackers lack exteroceptive feedback hence reacting to the environment remains the responsibility of a higher-level planner. Existing perceptive controllers train geometry-only encoders from scratch, trading semantics for sim-to-real ease, and typically rely on teacher-student distillation for a task of interest. We present ViBe, a post-training framework for adapting motion trackers to perceptive control tasks. We leverage pre-trained visual encoders with a multi-query extractor module to learn task-relevant perceptive feedback. This feedback is grafted onto the tracker's input via low-rank adapters, enabling parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Given a task reward and a reference dataset, this modular controller can be adapted directly via policy optimization. Across four tasks, ViBe shows zero-shot sim-to-real transfer spanning perceptive walking on curbs and parkour, Repose Cube, omni-object loco-manipulation, and dodgeball, with visually robust performance across outdoor, low-light, and RGB distractor conditions. Finally, we solve a goal-oriented Repose Cube task with a deliberately simple planner, demonstrating the efficacy of perceptive controllers, adapted by our approach.

</details>


### [99] [HaWMPO: Hallucination-Aware World Model-based Policy Optimization for Generalist Robot Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09941)
*Zengjue Chen,Peidong Liu,Jiawei Li,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HaWMPO：一种闭环强化学习流程，用幻觉感知世界模型对VLA策略进行后训练，抑制不可靠动作片段，提升长程操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在复杂长程场景中成功率有限；真实机器人在线强化学习成本高、样本效率低且存在硬件与安全风险；世界模型虽可替代真实交互进行想象rollout优化，但长程预测易产生幻觉，导致状态转移偏差并误导策略学习。

Method: 提出HaWMPO闭环强化学习流程：引入动作条件下的幻觉感知模型评估生成图像序列的可靠性，并将幻觉分数通过Reward-Soft机制融入组相对策略优化（GRPO），在训练中抑制不可靠的动作片段。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上取得最佳平均成功率，较基础模型提升15.0%，较最强基线提升2.8%；在G1机器人真实实验中，两个操作任务的平均成功率从67.5%提升至80.0%。

Conclusion: HaWMPO通过幻觉感知与Reward-Soft机制有效缓解世界模型长程rollout的预测偏差，为VLA策略提供高效、安全的离线后训练方案，并在仿真与真实场景中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies have demonstrated strong generalization across robotic manipulation tasks, yet their success rates remain limited in com- plex long-horizon scenarios. Recent methods improve Visual-Language-Action (VLA) policies through online reinforcement learning on real robots, but such training relies on costly physical interactions, suffers from low sample efficiency, and may introduce hardware and safety risks. World models offer a promising alternative by enabling policy optimization with imagined rollouts. However, long-horizon rollouts generated by world models often suffer from prediction hal- lucinations, producing biased state transitions that can mislead policy learning. To address this issue, we propose Hallucination-aware World Model-based Pol- icy Optimization (HaWMPO), a closed-loop reinforcement learning pipeline for VLA policy post-training with world models. Specifically, HaWMPO introduces an action-conditioned hallucination-aware model to estimate the reliability of gen- erated image sequences, and incorporates hallucination scores into group relative policy optimization through a Reward-Soft mechanism, suppressing unreliable ac- tion chunks during training. On the LIBERO benchmark, HaWMPO achieves the best average success rate, with gains of 15.0% over the base model and 2.8% over the strongest baseline; real-world experiments on a G1 robot further validate its effectiveness, raising the average success rate on two manipulation tasks from 67.5% to 80.0%.

</details>


### [100] [RoboDrop: Curating VLA Post-Training Data via Local Gradient Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10021)
*Runze Xu,Yuanfan Xu,Cuijie Xu,Shuang Dai,Yining Li,Yu Wang,Jincheng Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboDrop，一种基于训练轨迹中局部梯度兼容性的数据筛选框架，用于清理机器人数据集中的异构错误，提升 VLA 模型后训练的数据质量与策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型虽经大规模预训练获得泛化能力，但在适应新任务与机器人载体时仍需后训练，而后训练对数据质量高度敏感。实际采集的机器人数据常包含执行错误、传感器漂移、时间戳错位等异构错误，会损害后训练效果。人工检查成本高，现有数据清理方法多针对特定损坏类型，难以通用。

Method: RoboDrop 通过沿训练轨迹测量的局部梯度兼容性来审计监督信号，作为其对后训练性能影响的代理指标。在一次单轮预热训练中，将每个候选样本的梯度与任务语义匹配和视觉匹配的验证样本梯度进行比较，在线打分；随后在 episode 层面聚合样本分数，并用简单的自动后处理规则转化为过滤决策。

Result: 在受控的观测—动作损坏、仿真中自然次优演示以及包含非专家采集错误的真实机器人数据集上评估，RoboDrop 比现有方法更准确地识别不可靠演示。使用筛选后数据后训练，下游策略性能持续提升，真实机器人 rollout 平均成功率从 35.0% 提升到 67.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于训练轨迹感知、上下文条件化的监督审计是稳健 VLA 后训练的有效方法。

Abstract: Vision--language--action (VLA) models acquire broad generalization through large-scale pretraining, yet adapting them to a new task and robot embodiment still requires post-training on newly collected data. Unlike pretraining, post-training targets task- and embodiment-specific adaptation, making it particularly sensitive to data quality. In practice, collected robot datasets often contain heterogeneous errors, including execution mistakes, sensor drift, and timestamp misalignment, which can impair post-training and policy performance. Manual inspection is costly, while existing data-cleaning methods are typically tailored to particular corruption types. To address these challenges, we introduce \textsc{RoboDrop}, a data-curation framework that audits supervision using local gradient compatibility measured along the training trajectory as a proxy for its effect on post-training performance. During a one-epoch warm-up run, RoboDrop scores each candidate sample online by comparing its gradient with those of task-semantic and visually matched validation samples. The resulting sample scores are aggregated at the episode level, and a simple automatic post-processing rule converts them into filtering decisions. We evaluate RoboDrop on controlled observation--action corruptions, naturally suboptimal demonstrations in simulation, and real-robot datasets containing non-expert collection errors. Across these settings, RoboDrop more accurately distinguishes unreliable demonstrations than prior methods, while post-training on the curated data consistently yields stronger downstream policies, with average real-robot rollout success rising from $35.0\%$ to $67.5\%$. These results establish training-trajectory-aware, context-conditioned supervision auditing as an effective approach to robust VLA post-training.

</details>


### [101] [AXON: A ROS 2 RMW with Shared-Memory/QUIC Transport and QKD/ML-KEM Key Establishment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10024)
*Sergio Sánchez de la Fuente,Miguel Ángel González-Santamarta,Francisco Javier Rodríguez-Lera,Vicente Matellán Olivera,Ángel Manuel Guerrero-Higueras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AXON：一种按部署范围分离传输策略的ROS 2 RMW替代实现，同主机用POSIX共享内存环，远程用QUIC，并给出两种fail-closed的TLS 1.3密钥建立配置（混合后量子与QKD外置PSK）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS 2通过RMW接口抽象中间件，参考实现基于DDS；作者希望提供一种替代RMW方案，按部署范围（同主机/远程）拆分传输策略，并面向远程流量提供fail-closed的密钥建立选项，包括后量子混合与前量子密钥分发（QKD）外部PSK路径。

Method: AXON由Rust核心与C++适配器组成：同主机通信使用POSIX共享内存环，远程通信使用QUIC，daemon负责发现与图同步。给出两种TLS 1.3密钥建立配置：classic仅提供混合X25519MLKEM768组，拒绝协商纯经典组；qkd通过ETSI GS QKD 014 API导入256位密钥作为成对外部PSK，且不提供DH组。默认messages10策略额外用AES-256-GCM保护远程应用消息，每十条出站消息轮换KME材料，每个envelope使用新nonce；session仅依赖QUIC保护。外部PSK路径需对rustls做窄扩展，已随AXON打包。文中定义威胁模型，区分两种配置下的对等方认证。

Result: 实现了AXON原型及其rustls扩展，明确了两种密钥建立配置的认证语义，并界定了实现级验证的范围。文中未给出ROS 2一致性、对比性能与物理QKD验证的结论。

Conclusion: AXON展示了按部署范围分离传输策略的ROS 2 RMW替代路径，并提供fail-closed的远程密钥建立（后量子混合或QKD外置PSK）选项；其验证仅限实现层面，ROS 2一致性、性能对比与物理QKD验证留待后续。

Abstract: Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2) standardizes application code against a middleware interface (RMW) whose reference implementations are built on the Data Distribution Service (DDS). We present AXON, an alternative ROS 2 RMW implementation that separates transport policy by deployment scope. A Rust core and C++ adapter use POSIX shared-memory rings for same-host communication, QUIC for remote communication, and a daemon for discovery and graph synchronization. We then describe two fail-closed TLS 1.3 key-establishment configurations for remote traffic. The classic configuration offers only the hybrid X25519MLKEM768 group, preventing negotiation of a classical-only group. The qkd configuration imports a 256-bit key obtained through the ETSI GS QKD 014 API as a pairwise external PSK and offers no Diffie-Hellman group. Its default messages10 strategy additionally protects remote application messages with AES-256-GCM, rotating KME material after ten outgoing messages and using a fresh nonce per envelope; session relies on QUIC protection alone. The external-PSK path requires a narrow extension to rustls, now bundled with AXON. We define the threat model, distinguish peer authentication in the two configurations, and delimit the implementation-level validation from ROS 2 conformance, comparative performance, and physical-QKD validation.

</details>


### [102] [What Symmetry Buys a Learned Motion Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10033)
*Andrea Emir Sevincel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出用起止点(s, g)确定的封闭形式坐标系来表达轨迹与障碍物，从而在查询时为规划器免费提供SE(3)中的3个平移和2个旋转等变性，仅剩绕起止轴的一个旋转无法连续消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的运动规划器在训练时代价高昂，且在以世界坐标训练时，会在每个位置和姿态上重复学习相同运动。现有方法分别在训练数据、推理算子或网络权重中恢复缺失的刚体等变性，但各有代价。作者想探究规划查询本身能免费提供多少这种等变性。

Method: 利用起点s与终点g以封闭形式确定一个坐标系：原点在中点，第一轴沿g-s方向。在该坐标系中表达轨迹与障碍物，每次查询仅需一次叉乘，即可在初始化时消除SE(3)的3个平移与2个旋转，且对架构无任何约束。对残余的绕起止轴旋转，作者构建了三种机制，并辅以非对称干预进行校准对比。

Result: 在拥挤的3D基准上，固定架构、数据与预算，该坐标系将留出的无碰撞率从14.60%提升到51.10%，而起点到终点的直线段仅为15.6%，世界坐标模型甚至无法超过直线段。三种针对残余旋转的机制各自提升不足1个百分点，尽管等变主干网络达到给定水平快2-3倍。两个非等变残差难以区分的模型实际相差28个百分点；经非对称干预校准后，局部几何的贡献(+40.0)甚至大于坐标系(+36.5)。

Conclusion: 表示本身提供的等变性远超过任何机制所强制施加的等变性，且标准等变性诊断指标无法察觉这一差异。坐标系并非最大的可用效应，局部几何的收益更大，提示规划性能的关键在于表示选择而非等变机制设计。

Abstract: Learning-based motion planners pay at training what classical planners pay per query. Trained in world coordinates, they relearn the same motion at every position and orientation. Existing work restores the missing rigid-body equivariance in the training data, in the inference operator, or in the weights, and each carries a cost. We ask how much of that equivariance the planning query supplies for free.
  A start s and a goal g determine a frame in closed form, with origin at their midpoint and first axis along g-s. Expressing trajectory and obstacles in that frame removes three translations and two rotations of SE(3), at initialisation, for one cross product per query and with no constraint on the architecture. A single rotation about the start-goal axis remains, and no continuous rule removes it. On a cluttered 3D benchmark, holding architecture, data and budget fixed, the frame raises the held-out collision-free rate from 14.60% to 51.10%, where a straight segment from start to goal scores 15.6% and the world-frame model does not beat it. We build all three mechanisms for the residual rotation and each is worth under a point, though the equivariant backbone reaches any given level two to three times sooner. What the representation supplies therefore dominates what any mechanism enforces, and the standard diagnostic does not see the difference: two models with indistinguishable non-equivariance residuals differ by 28 points. Calibrated against a non-symmetry intervention, the frame is not even the largest effect available, since local geometry is worth +40.0 where the frame is worth +36.5.

</details>


### [103] [Grounding Generated Video Plans in Simulation Towards Versatile Dexterous Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10050)
*Tianyue Wu,Boyuan An,Shuqi Zhao,Heyu Guo,Wanli Xing,Yi Ma,Kaifeng Zhang,Ruihai Wu,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将生成的手-物交互视频与基于仿真的HOI grounding结合，用生成视频提供多样参考运动，训练多物体多轨迹跟踪器，成功在仿真中grounding超过1,500个生成视频，并在真实世界实验中实现多样抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于仿真的HOI跟踪可将运动学参考转化为可行的底层控制，但其可扩展性受限于缺乏可靠的参考运动。生成视频虽能提供可控的操作运动提案，但单独使用难以保证物理可行性，因此需要将生成视频与仿真grounding结合。

Method: 提出一种通过HOI重建实现可扩展参考生成的方法，仅需极少人工干预。训练阶段，利用生成视频为学习多物体、多轨迹的HOI跟踪器提供多样运动参考；部署阶段，由视频模型生成运动规划，再交由学得的跟踪器执行。

Result: 在仿真中成功grounding超过1,500个生成视频，仿真训练中的成功率比基线高出25个百分点以上。在真实世界闭环实验中，实现了多样抓取，包括功能性抓取、非抓握式操作以及抓取后物体位姿跟踪。

Conclusion: 生成视频与仿真HOI grounding的结合能够有效扩展HOI跟踪的训练参考来源，并在仿真与真实世界中取得优于基线的表现，代码与视频已公开。

Abstract: Generated hand-object interaction (HOI) videos provide a controllable way to propose manipulation motions. Simulation-based HOI tracking can translate such kinematic references into feasible low-level control, but its scalability is limited by the lack of reliable reference motions. We therefore combine generated videos with simulation-based HOI grounding: during training, generated videos provide diverse motion references for learning a multi-object, multi-trajectory HOI tracker, and at deployment, the video model produces motion plans that are executed by the learned tracker. In particular, we propose a method that enables scalable reference generation by HOI reconstruction with minimal manual intervention and successfully grounds more than 1,500 generated videos in simulation, achieving success rates over 25 percentage points higher than those of baselines during simulation-based training. In real-world closed-loop experiments, it achieves diverse grasps, including functional grasps, non-prehensile manipulation, and post-grasp object-pose tracking. Videos and code are available at https://boyuan-an.github.io/GALATEA/.

</details>


### [104] [Automatic Reproducible Camera Intrinsic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10082)
*Xiangcheng Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种全自动相机内参标定流程，自动剔除低质量图像并选择径向畸变阶数，在美国自有数据与五个公开数据集上取得最低留出重投影误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机内参标定精度依赖所采集图像的质量，但现有基于标靶的方法通常需要人工剔除低质量图像，并人工指定合适的径向畸变阶数，缺乏自动化手段。

Method: 采用迭代剔除策略：在候选图像集上估计参数，剔除平均残差超过中位数若干倍的视图；该过程在每个候选畸变阶数下独立运行，使保留图像集与该阶数的残差尺度一致。畸变阶数则在留出图像上选择，固定内参与畸变，仅重新估计标靶位姿，以保证新增系数有独立观测支持。最后将两步整合为交互式标定工具，支持全流程数据检查与参数估计。

Result: 在自有相机数据与五个公开真实数据集上，图像筛选使留出重投影误差降低25%，阶数选择再降低5%，在四种对比配置中取得最低的留出平均误差，且无需人工选图。

Conclusion: 该全自动标定流程可同时自动完成图像筛选与畸变阶数选择，显著提升标定精度，作者将公开代码与数据以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Accurate camera intrinsic calibration is fundamental to robot perception, and the accuracy depends on the quality of the collected images. However, existing target-based calibration methods often require the practitioner to manually filter out high-quality images and to specify an appropriate radial distortion order. This paper presents a fully automatic intrinsic calibration pipeline that determines both from the collected data. We adopt an iterative rejection scheme that estimates parameters on a candidate image set and removes views whose mean residual exceeds a multiple of the median. Crucially, this process runs independently under each candidate distortion order, so that the retained image set is consistent with the residual scale of that order. Further, the distortion order is selected on held-out images, with the intrinsics and distortion fixed and only the board pose re-estimated, ensuring that an added coefficient is supported by independent observations. Finally, we integrate both steps into an interactive calibration tool that supports full-pipeline data inspection and parameter estimation. Experiments on our own camera data and five public real-world datasets show that image filtering reduces the held-out reprojection error by 25\%, the order selection further by 5\%, achieving the lowest held-out mean among four compared configurations without manual image selection. We will release the code and data to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [105] [Assembling Two Parts in One Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10137)
*Liuao Pei,Tianyue Wu,Hui Zhang,Ping Luo,Jie Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究单只灵巧手内的指级协作，通过强化学习实现在手内拼装两个刚性物体，无需第二只手臂或夹具，并在三个任务上实现从仿真到硬件的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类灵巧性的标志之一是不同手指承担不同但协调的角色以完成精细操作（如用手持笔身盖笔帽）。作者希望通过"在手内拼装"这一任务研究手指级别的协作，并探索其对机械手形态的要求。

Method: 提出统一的强化学习框架，以两部件间的目标相对位姿作为驱动目标；通过基于函数的辅助奖励塑造手指协作，并正则化至单一人类参考姿态；采用域随机化，并融合历史本体感觉与物体观测以应对遮挡导致的估计噪声。

Result: 同一套方案成功求解三个不同拼装任务（Bottle、Syringe、Marker）；仅在仿真中训练的策略可零样本迁移到配备单相机的真实硬件，展现出对遮挡引起状态估计误差的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在手内拼装对手部形态提出较高要求，可作为现代机器人手系统的基准测试；视频与代码已公开。

Abstract: A hallmark of human dexterity is the cooperative use of fingers, where different fingers take on distinct yet coordinated roles to accomplish fine manipu- lation, such as capping a pen with the hand that holds it. We study this finger-level coordination through in-hand assembly: mating two rigid objects within a single dexterous hand, with no second arm and no fixture. We present a reinforcement learning formulation to solve this problem in a unified framework, which is driven by a goal relative pose between the two parts. Finger coordination is shaped by a function-based auxiliary reward and regularized toward a single human reference pose, while domain randomization and a fusion of historical proprioception and object observation confer robustness to occlusion-induced estimation noise. The same recipe solves three different assembly tasks (Bottle, Syringe, and Marker). Trained purely in simulation, the policies transfer zero-shot to hardware with a single camera, demonstrating robustness to state-estimation errors caused by oc- clusion. Our experiments also reveal that in-hand assembly places demands on hand morphology and can serve as a benchmark for modern robotic hand systems. Videos and code are available at https://ltbgbird.github.io/in-hand-assembly-page/.

</details>


### [106] [Future-Aware Flow Planning for Safe UAV Target Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10166)
*Boning Feng,Haoran Zhang,Xiaowen Bi,Yanzhen Zhang,Xiaodan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向无人机目标跟随的未来感知流规划框架，用预测目标未来引导轨迹生成，并在采样循环内嵌风险评分的前缀修复，在仿真基准上改善了安全-跟踪权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱环境中无人机目标跟随本质上是预测性问题：仅依赖当前状态的跟随者容易在转弯时滞后、误选受阻通道，或在跟踪与不安全近地平线运动之间被迫取舍。因此需要将目标未来信息纳入规划。

Method: 预测的目标未来以"地平线对齐的残差信号"形式引导干净的无人机轨迹生成；同时将带风险评分的"可执行前缀修复"嵌入采样循环内部。通过消融验证未来自适应与安全修复的各自贡献，并开展面向仿真器的压力测试考察接口、感知与控制器执行的影响。

Result: 在固定ID/OOD滚动地平线基准上，规划器改善而非全面主导安全-跟踪权衡：ID碰撞率与之一致为零且ID安全跟踪时间最高；OOD宏观碰撞率和最终跟踪误差在所展示方法中最低。但Future-MPC在平滑度及部分阈值化OOD成功率指标上更优。消融显示未来自适应在安全修复前即改善候选生成。

Conclusion: 地平线对齐的未来自适应与嵌入式前缀修复是互补要素，在上述仿真条件下有助于实现安全的无人机目标跟随；结论限于所测试的仿真场景。

Abstract: UAV target following in cluttered environments is inherently predictive: current-state followers can lag behind turns, choose blocked corridors, or trade tracking for unsafe near-horizon motion. We propose a future-aware flow planning framework for state-informed UAV target following. Predicted target futures guide clean UAV trajectory generation as horizon-aligned residual signals, while risk-scored executable-prefix repair is embedded inside the sampling loop. On fixed ID/OOD receding-horizon benchmarks, the planner improves the intended safety--tracking trade-off rather than dominating every metric: it matches zero measured ID collision rate with the highest ID safe-tracking time, and gives the lowest OOD macro collision rate and final tracking error among the displayed methods, while Future-MPC remains smoother and stronger on some thresholded OOD success metrics under its hand-designed objective. Ablations show that future adaptation improves candidate generation before safety repair, and simulator-facing stress tests probe interface, sensing, and controller-execution effects. These results support horizon-aligned future adaptation and embedded prefix repair as complementary ingredients for safe UAV target following under the tested simulation conditions.

</details>


### [107] [Multi-Robot Scanner for Automated Full-Body Dermoscopic Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10169)
*Valerio Franchi,Rafael Garcia,Nuno Gracias,Ricard Campos,Josep Quintana,Sandra González-Villà,Mark Ventura,Nuria Ferrera,Clément Lenoir,Josep Malvehy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文设计并实现了一套由四台UR10机械臂末端相机组成的全身成像扫描仪，可自动、非接触地拍摄达到皮肤镜级别的皮肤病变图像，性能优于现有全身摄影系统Vectra，接近接触式皮肤镜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身摄影（total-body photography）与手持皮肤镜（dermoscopy）之间存在成像质量与自动化程度的差距：全身摄影视野广但分辨率不足，皮肤镜分辨率高但需人工接触式检查，难以融入常规筛查流程。因此需要一种能自动、非接触获取皮肤镜级别图像的全身扫描系统。

Method: 在四台UR10机械臂的末端执行器上安装相机，构建全身成像扫描仪；系统包含视角规划算法（为痣选择最佳相机位置）、高层控制器（使多机械臂协同工作）以及碰撞检测器（接触物体或人时停止机械臂）。通过真实患者全身扫描进行评估，将图像与接触式皮肤镜及现有全身摄影系统Vectra在临床相关病变特征上比较，并使用USAF 1951分辨率靶量化真实光学分辨率。

Result: 扫描仪最小可分辨特征尺寸为22.1微米，接触式皮肤镜为8.8微米；在多数临床相关特征上，扫描仪持续优于Vectra，并在大部分评估特征上与接触式皮肤镜表现相当。

Conclusion: 该扫描仪可自动、非接触地获取皮肤镜质量图像，无需额外的人工皮肤镜检查，缩小了全身摄影与手持皮肤镜之间的差距，显示出未来融入筛查工作流程的潜力。

Abstract: This paper outlines the specifications and design approach used to construct a full body imaging scanner capable of capturing skin lesions at a dermatoscopic level using cameras mounted on the end-effectors of four UR10 manipulators.
